### greet-zh

### direct-ja

```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **merge** cuando quieres conservar el historial tal cual ocurrió, sobre todo en ramas compartidas o ya publicadas. Usa **rebase** para limpiar tu rama local antes de integrarla (reaplicar tus commits sobre la última versión de la base), siempre que no la hayas subido o nadie más trabaje sobre ella.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、保证可靠有序送达；UDP 无连接、不保证送达，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when your branch is private and not yet shared, to tidy your commits into a clean line before you open a PR. Merge when others already work from that branch, since rebasing rewrites history they depend on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```
bun test test/user.test.ts
```

只写一部分名字也行，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段文字的测试文件，比如 `bun test user` 会跑所有路径里带 `user` 的测试。

如果想只跑某一个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```
bun test test/user.test.ts -t "登录失败"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data files. If the system crashes, it replays that log on restart to recover any committed changes that hadn't reached the data files yet.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着那个 compaction 的 PR 把锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到这个 PR 的 review 意见，看清楚它说的锁粒度问题是什么。
- 把锁的粒度改细，只改 review 点到的部分，不借机改别的逻辑。
- 跑一遍相关测试，把结果贴出来作为证据。
- 改动提交在你自己的分支上。

不要做的：
- 不推送，不合并，不把 draft 改成 ready，不在 PR 上评论。这些等用户说 Go。

完成的标准：锁粒度按 review 意见改细了，相关测试通过，报告里说清楚改了哪几处、怎么验证的。
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五京沪 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">2 班</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">G1、G3 都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">每天的班次</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<p class="k-sub" style="color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px">每天早上自动更新。</p>
<script>
(function(){
  var fallback = ducter.get("fallback") || [];
  function render(trains){
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    var under = trains.filter(function(t){return t.price < 600;});
    document.getElementById('cnt').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = under.length
      ? under.map(function(t){return t.no + ' ' + t.price + ' 元';}).join('、')
      : '暂时没有 600 元以下的';
    trains.forEach(function(t){
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + t.no + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + t.seats + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value" style="color:' + (t.price < 600 ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-ink)') + '">' + t.price + ' 元</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  var saved = null;
  try { saved = ducter.get('trains'); } catch(e) {}
  Promise.resolve(saved).then(function(v){
    render(Array.isArray(v) && v.length ? v : fallback);
  });
  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(function(v){
      if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) render(v);
    });
  });
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张","under":true},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张","under":true},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座","under":false}],"fallback":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张","under":true},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张","under":true},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座","under":false}]}

  .k-sum-sub{font-size:14px;color:var(--d-soft)}
</style>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (Number(x.yuan) || 0), 0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + total.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + Number(x.yuan).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
ducter.summary("3 笔,合计 ¥1,966");
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="t">时间</label><input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="HH:MM"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="ml">奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal"></div>

  .k-row{cursor:pointer}
  .k-del{margin-left:8px}
</style>
<script>
  const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  const now = new Date();
  const today = now.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(now.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(now.getDate());
  let feeds = [];
  let sel = -1;

  function render() {
    const todays = feeds
      .map((f, i) => ({ ...f, i }))
      .filter(f => f.date === today)
      .sort((a, b) => (a.time < b.time ? -1 : 1));
    const total = todays.reduce((s, f) => s + Number(f.ml), 0);
    const totalEl = document.getElementById('total');
    cons … odays.length + ' 次,合计 ' + total + ' ml');
  }

  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      feeds.splice(Number(del.dataset.del), 1);
      sel = -1;
      ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
      render();
      return;
    }
    const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (row) {
      const i = Number(row.dataset.i);
      sel = sel === i ? -1 : i;
      render();
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
    const ml = Number(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    feeds.push({ date: today, time: t, ml: ml });
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
    document.getElementById('t').value = pad(new Date().getHours()) + ':' + pad(new Date().getMinutes());
    render();
  });

  document.getElementById('t').value = pad(now.getHours()) + ':' + pad(now.getMinutes());

  ducter.onChange(() => {
    feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
    render();
  });
  feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf with the four PRs, and each one gets ticked off as it lands.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2067 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still open</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="n">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">Tick each one off when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
const items = ducter.get("items") || [];
let done = {};

function render() {
  const list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.textContent = '';
  let open = 0;
  items.forEach(function (it) {
    const landed = !!done[it.id];
    if (!landed) open++;
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = 'k-row';
    const label = document.createElement('label');
    label.style.cssText = 'display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;width:100%;cursor:pointer';
    const cb = document.createElement('input');
    cb.type = 'checkbox';
    cb.checked = landed;
    cb.style.cssText = 'margin-top:3px;width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:var(--d-accent)';
    cb.onchange = function () {
      done[it.id] = cb.checked;
      ducter.set('landed', done);
    };
    const main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.style.cssText = 'flex:1;min-width:0;' + (landed ? 'text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)' : '');
    main.textContent = it.title;
    const sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = landed ? 'Landed' : it.sub;
    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.style.cssText = 'flex:1;min-width:0';
    wrap.appendChild(main);
    wrap.appendChild(sub);
    label.appendChild(cb);
    label.appendChild(wrap);
    li.appendChild(label);
    list.appendChild(li);
  });
  document.getElementById('n').textContent = String(open);
  document.getElementById('s').textContent = open === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tick each one off when it lands';
  ducter.summary(open + ' open');
}

async function load() {
  done = (await ducter.get('landed')) || {};
  render();
}

ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut · wey-gu/ducter #412","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Mon"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502 · nowledge-co/mem #10287","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test · nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tue"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar · wey-gu/ducter #418","sub":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一台米白色的卡通笔记本电脑，3D 质感，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色。

适合放在空状态里。屏幕空着正好对应"还没有内容"，米白色也比较柔和，不会让人觉得冷清或出错。用的时候配一句说明和一个操作按钮效果最好，比如"还没有任何记录"加一个"新建"。

要注意两点：深色边框在深色模式下可能显得突兀，最好测一下；它比较小（320×246），放大后会模糊，建议用原始矢量或更高分辨率的版本。

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我在 TTL 测试里补一条正好到期那一刻的用例，弄好了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

要求：在 TTL 测试里补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻（假时钟推进到恰好等于 TTL 的时刻）。需要确认：到期那一刻的值是已过期（取不到），到期前一刻仍有效。沿用现在的假时钟写法，不要用真实 sleep 或真实计时。

完成标准：新测试通过；整套 TTL 测试连续跑 20 次全过；在分支上提交，并把测试输出报告出来。

不在范围内：不合并，不推送，不开 PR。

地方：~/dev/foo（分支 ducter/foo-ttl）。
```
